# Credits and license audit — October 2, 2026

- Four PDF.js fonts use GPL v2; 14 fonts need an allowlist decision (F5).
- Bunny copyright and derived-asset change notices need fixes (F1).
- Missing records include seven models and 344 generated images (F2).
- The sound catalog omits the sources of 69 of 85 shipped MP3s (F3).
- Live checks blocked: 39 flag pages and seven other Commons pages (F4).
- Inventory: 772 asset files, 234 vendor files, 10 direct dependencies.
- No selected manifest output is missing; candidate records are separate.
- All 85 MP3 pages show CC0; all 15 SuperSplat scenes show CC BY 4.0.
- Verified upstream identities are documented; other identities are unverified.

## Scope and method

Codex built this report on `codex/credits-audit`, cut from `main` at
`2ec27c1568cdd496f6fc9d8416be8b5049ee4fe5`. Only this report is changed. An unrelated session
switched the shared checkout to another audit branch, so this audit continued in an isolated
worktree at the same baseline. It did not alter that session's files or branch.

The denominator is every tracked file below `assets/` and `vendor/`, including labs-only, unused
variants, JSON sidecars, fonts, license files and thumbnails. The filesystem walk and
`git ls-files assets vendor` both returned the same 1,006 files. Captures includes scientific and
text data and video-trained splats; meshes includes sampled mesh outputs and character sidecars;
images and fonts includes media samples, texture sidecars and PDF.js fonts. Every file appears in
exactly one of the five tables. Assets embedded directly in `src/`, outside these directories, are
supplementary checks rather than additions to this denominator.

Source pages were opened with direct public HTTP GETs on October 2, 2026; the browser/search reader
was also used to cross-check source and license pages. A successful GET alone was not counted as a
license confirmation: the file-specific license, author, title and offered download were inspected.
For Poly Haven, the asset page, current `info` and `files` API responses and
[CC0 policy](https://polyhaven.com/license) were opened. For SuperSplat, all 15 scene pages show a
linked CC BY 4.0 notice and enabled download; the manifest's source metadata URLs also opened.
[Dany Bittel's profile](https://superspl.at/user/danylyon) confirms `danylyon`, and
[YUMA's profile](https://superspl.at/user/yyouzhen) confirms `yyouzhen`; these aliases are not an
author mismatch.

**Status meaning:** “OK” applies only to the evidence stated in that row. “Identity not checked”
means a conversion's configured source was traced, but the shipped output was not reproduced from a
verified original. Current pages cannot prove an acquisition-date license without an acquisition
receipt; no independent historical receipts were available. A rate limit is an access limit, not a
dead link or evidence of a changed license. Cached search-reader copies of some blocked Commons
pages were readable, but do not replace a current HTTP check.

No recorded or checked content asset is CC BY-SA. The task's per-asset BY-SA notice and inheritance
condition therefore has no positive content example to exercise here. The Wikimedia page footer's
BY-SA terms apply to page text, not automatically to a flag or image. If a later check finds a BY-SA
file, its own file notice must determine the version, and both its transformed output and thumbnail
must retain that license with a notice beside it, under
[CC BY-SA 4.0's terms](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/) and CLAUDE.md. The live
Wikipedia fetch is explicitly exempted in CLAUDE.md and is not a shipped file.

## Findings

### F1 — Preserve required attribution and identify modifications

[Big Buck Bunny's source policy](https://peach.blender.org/about/) gives a specific attribution for
reused portions: the 2008 Blender Foundation copyright notice and `www.bigbuckbunny.org`.
`src/packs/screens.js` gives the title, Blender Foundation, a source link and CC BY 3.0 link, but
omits that notice. CREDITS.md also omits the copyright year. Fix both MP4/WebM credits. The in-app
author/license/source fields exist for the other BY content examined.

[CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/) requires identifying changes;
[CC BY 3.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/) also requires appropriate credit and
identifying adaptations. `src/video3d/samples.js` names the two video titles and excerpt lengths,
but its rendered credits do not identify the conversion into trained splats and a solved camera
path. `worlds/index.html` names 100STYLE and its linked CC BY 4.0 license but does not identify
retargeting, loop cuts or in-place playback. Those changes are documented only in
CREDITS.md/manifest/builder. Put a short change notice in each in-app credit.

`src/app.js:renderCredits` renders title, source, author and linked license for recipe credits; it
does not render a `changes` field on them. For future lane fixes, either put the change notice in a
field this renderer already displays or have the engine lane render it. Do not assume a newly added
`changes` property is visible. Existing real-object credits already describe paint-outs/cuts in
their titles, and microscopy/galaxy credits identify subsets. Captured-toy credits render their
existing `changes` fields.

### F2 — Complete individual asset records and license declarations

The selected manifests cover 33 packed toys (including two retained chess scan files now unused by
the kit chess toy), 15 Poly Haven source models, 85 MP3s, two sample GLBs, three photo/depth pairs,
two video-splat/camera pairs, five science samples, and the Worlds source groups. None of those
expected output files is missing. Their source `.cache/` paths describe intermediate build inputs,
not deployment files; absent cached inputs are not orphan assets.

Seven `.splats` files use `tools/ro-sources.mjs` instead of either named JSON build manifest. Their
current Sketchfab/Poly Haven pages and pack credits agree with CREDITS.md, but the task's manifest
layer is absent. Four protein PDBs, the GloVe word subset, eight Flickr sample photos, the Screen's
three media files, the Gaussian-splatting strawberry image, the sample article PDF and generated
sample WAV likewise have no individual entry in the named manifests. The Worlds person-splat binary
and sidecar are not mapped in assets.worlds, which points the MakeHuman group only at
`assets/worlds/character/`.

All 341 thumbnails and three app icons lack individual manifest/CREDITS.md entries. A toy's credit
does not explicitly declare its thumbnail's license. Record source-license inheritance for
source-derived renders and an allowed explicit license/author for original renders/icons. The
original sample article's asset license and any embedded fonts are not separately recorded. Do not
silently treat original assets as CC0 because Splashery's software is MIT.

The first four `assets.toys` records have no author field or author in their source note; Cactus
also omits its CC0 license. The 15 model-backed records can resolve author/license through
models.json; the other captured entries carry prose source notes. Add structured `author`,
`license`, `licenseUrl` and page fields, while preserving exact source URLs and conversion details.
Add output hashes/versions and individual derived-file mappings so a future audit can distinguish a
source-page match from an artifact identity check.

The protein pack credits only wwPDB and “the structures' authors,” while CREDITS.md lists the
individual structure authors. CC0 does not require attribution, but that is not the task's requested
three-record agreement. Carry the individual names and structure pages into each sample's manifest
and in-app credit.

### F3 — The sound-source catalog describes candidates, not selected outputs

Every one of the 85 MP3s has a selected `assets.soundSamples` entry, a CREDITS.md entry (including
the grouped piano notes), and a SOUND_CREDITS entry. All selected author/license fields agree with
the in-app records, and the live source pages show CC0 1.0. The deleted Freesound account remains
visibly identified as `deleted_user_3544904`; it is not a dead link. Piano credits use a pack URL
while the selected manifest gives each individual note URL; that is a scope difference, not a
contradictory source.

`tools/sound-sources.json` has 252 candidate records for 102 toy keys, representing 210 unique
sources. Only 15 unique sources overlap the 83 unique selected MP3 source URLs (after normalizing
Freesound short links and full links). This accounts for 16 of 85 MP3s; **69 shipped files have no
corresponding catalog source**. The other 195 unique catalog sources have no selected shipped
recording. Their `file` values are remote preview URLs, not local output promises; they should be
marked as candidates, not reported as 195 missing MP3s. Each affected shipped file is marked F3 in
the sounds table.

Keep this catalog's candidate role explicit and add an authoritative selected-source section with
output file, exact source ID/preview or archive member, author, license/version, cut, encoding and
source/output hashes. In particular, Sound B's selected files need to replace or coexist explicitly
with its earlier candidate choices. Do not delete candidate research as part of a license repair
without a lane decision. `src/toy-sounds.js` and the instrument sample expansion in `src/voices.js`
trace the shipped recordings; `src/app.js` uses `samplesIn(..., true)` and SOUND_CREDITS to render
the applicable credit.

### F4 — Keep access and identity limits visible

157 flag pages opened and each offered a file-specific public-domain or CC0 option; 39 returned
HTTP 429. Seven non-flag Commons file pages also returned HTTP 429: the pencil, Forest Away Path,
Still Life with Cheese, Muybridge horse GIF, strawberry photo, Liberty video and Edinburgh video.
All affected rows preserve the recorded claim and say not checked. Do not call these dead links,
license changes or confirmed allowed files until checked live.

All 196 flag entries have an SVG; no extra SVG or missing flag file was found. The recorded byte
lengths match all local SVGs. Original remote SVG byte identity was not checked.
`tools/fetch-flags.mjs` cuts author strings to 160 characters and substitutes a generic contributor
label when absent. Haiti and Lithuania demonstrate truncation; Guinea-Bissau's live page lacks
author information rather than naming the generic fallback. Preserve full source author text, and
represent missing author information explicitly. The selected flag already has a source link and
license text in src/app.js, but omits its author. Public-domain/CC0 flags do not require BY
attribution, so this is a record agreement gap, not evidence of a BY violation.

For file identity, the four unchanged protein PDBs matched current RCSB downloads byte for byte. The
Picture lab photo matched Flickr's offered image exactly. The seven resized Flickr photos match the
same picture at reduced dimensions: comparing both in 64×64 RGB produces mean channel error
0.216–0.672 on a 0–255 scale. This supports image identity without claiming an exact-byte match or a
reproduced resize command. The remaining transformed content is traced to builder inputs but lacks
independently verified hash lineage.

### F5 — Enumerate PDF.js resource licenses and resolve the asset-policy conflict

[The published pdfjs-dist 6.3.289 archive](https://registry.npmjs.org/pdfjs-dist/-/pdfjs-dist-6.3.289.tgz)
contains the exact vendored fonts and license files. The ten Foxit/PDFium PFB fonts are BSD
3-clause; the four LiberationSans TTF fonts use **GPL v2 with a font exception**, as the shipped
LICENSE_LIBERATION states. They are not Apache 2.0 and this particular bundle is not the newer OFL
font distribution. Full group license files ship locally, including the GPL text. However,
LICENSES.md omits both font licenses, the Adobe CMap BSD 3-clause notice and the ICC profile's CC0
notice. Its opening “two libraries / both MIT” statement is stale.

The font licenses lie outside the task's CC0/BY/BY-SA/public-domain content allowlist. CLAUDE.md
explicitly approves PDF.js and its vendored runtime, but does not expressly resolve these fonts
against the asset rule. **Policy conflict requires an owner/lane resolution**: use fonts under the
existing allowed asset licenses, or obtain an explicit documented rule for this approved runtime
resource bundle. This audit does not change the policy. If the GPL fonts remain, resolve
corresponding font source/distribution obligations: no corresponding font source or written source
offer was located in the repository. The document-embedding exception alone does not establish
permission to redistribute standalone TTF files without the other applicable terms. This is an
unresolved compliance check, not a finding that every PDF or the whole site becomes GPL.

All 10 direct package.json devDependencies are already listed with their license somewhere in
LICENSES.md. The published package metadata/licenses were checked at the pinned versions; jsfive's
license file expressly dedicates jsfive to the public domain while retaining its pyfive
origin/copyright notice. Build-only Blender/MPFB and external generation services are listed as
additional tools, not mistaken for direct npm dependencies or vendored site code.

### F6 — Check ONNX Runtime's upstream third-party notice obligations

The three vendored ONNX Runtime Web files match the published 1.30.0 package, and the
[MIT license](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/onnxruntime/main/LICENSE) matches the
local copy. The upstream repository also has a
[third-party notice bundle](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/onnxruntime/main/ThirdPartyNotices.txt),
which is absent under vendor/onnxruntime-web. That upstream document covers multiple builds; its
applicability to this WebAssembly-only binary was not determined. Have the lane inspect the exact
build's incorporated components and ship the applicable required notices before claiming that the
MIT file alone completes that binary's license obligations. This is a notice-completeness
uncertainty, not a confirmed missing notice for every component in the upstream bundle.

The 20 Splat.js modules and MIT license match the named upstream commit exactly; omggif's wrapper
preserves the original source and notice, and gifenc matches after the documented source-map-comment
removal. The 199 compared PDF.js package files match exactly. The model's SHA-256 is
`fcf51f1b230362b28690bb9d1809bf0431f29cad20534e3f589bd7285547f20d`, equal to its current Hugging
Face LFS metadata. The Small model's Apache 2.0 license is explicitly approved in CLAUDE.md; the
Base/Large/Giant model rules were not substituted for it.

## Reverse checks and supplementary source data

No expected file is absent among: the 33 toy output pairs (including looked-up variants), 15
mesh-source output pairs, 85 selected MP3 outputs, two sample GLBs, three photo/depth pairs, two
video-splat/camera pairs, five science samples, 196 flag files, or the shelf's asset URLs and
SOUND_CREDITS entries. Every toy has its thumbnail. Worlds collection records were matched through
their sidecars and build tools, rather than pretending each collection `out` directory enumerates
every derived file. F2 identifies the missing explicit mappings.

Data baked directly into JavaScript is outside the 1,006-file denominator but needs the same care.
The CNN training-data UCI page timed out and is **not checked**:
[Optical Recognition of Handwritten Digits](https://archive.ics.uci.edu/dataset/80/optical+recognition+of+handwritten+digits).
CREDITS.md and src/packs/computing.js record E. Alpaydin, C. Kaynak and CC BY 4.0; the in-app notice
does not explicitly identify trained weights/test examples as derived from that dataset. A lane
should add that change notice when closing F1. This audit did not retrain weights or establish
output identity. The NIST, PubChem, IUPAC and PDB chemistry source pages linked in CREDITS.md were
opened; that does not independently certify all embedded numerical values or settle rights in every
compiled table.

The report does not relicense any file, reconstruct absent original inputs, regenerate any asset,
change the selected sound catalog, or fix product code. All repairs below belong to the appropriate
lanes.

## Verification

- The filesystem and tracked inventory agree: 772 assets + 234 vendor files. The tables contain
  those 1,006 exact file paths once each, plus the 10 direct devDependencies.
- Selected manifest output, flag, thumbnail, toy URL and sound-credit reverse checks found zero
  missing files; catalog candidates and cached intermediates are treated separately.
- Live-source checks, alias checks, public package/archive comparisons, model hash check, RCSB byte
  comparisons and Flickr image comparisons are described above with limits.
- `npx prettier --check .`, `npx prettier --check docs/audits/credits-audit-2026-10.md`, and
  `node tools/us-english.mjs --diff` passed, with Prettier explicitly pinned to 3.8.1 using
  `npx --yes prettier@3.8.1`. `git diff --cached --check` also passed.
- No src/ file changes, asset builds or Playwright execution were needed for this report-only task.
  In-app credit behavior was inspected statically, not visually accepted in a browser.

## Exact fixes for the lanes

1. **src/packs/screens.js; CREDITS.md:** preserve the source-specified 2008 Blender Foundation
   copyright notice and bigbuckbunny.org attribution for both bunny clips; keep the linked CC BY 3.0
   license and say this is a cut excerpt. Add the three Screen output paths to tools/assets.json
   with exact source/version and output identity evidence.
2. **src/video3d/samples.js; worlds/index.html; src/packs/computing.js:** explicitly describe,
   respectively, video-to-splat/camera conversion, retargeted/cut motion loops, and trained CNN
   weights/test samples. Keep authors, source links and existing license links. If using structured
   change fields, an engine lane must update src/app.js:renderCredits to display recipe changes.
3. **tools/assets.json; tools/models.json:** add the seven .splats source/output records from
   tools/ro-sources.mjs; four assets/proteins/_.pdb records; word-vectors/words.txt; eight Flickr
   samples; the Gaussian-splatting strawberry.jpg; article.pdf; sample.wav; all 341 thumb.webp
   files; all three assets/app/icon-_.png files; and the two person-splats outputs. Keep companion
   sources and inheritance explicit. Add author/license/page fields to the first four packed-toy
   records, including Cactus's CC0 claim. Preserve existing conversions.
4. **CREDITS.md; src/packs/atoms.js:** enumerate each protein sample's structure-specific
   author/source/license in the manifest and rendered credit, matching the existing CREDITS.md
   names. In CREDITS.md, add thumbnail/icon provenance and explicit allowed licenses for original
   generated assets; revise the obsolete CC0/BY-only opening sentence to describe the current rule
   without claiming unchecked assets comply. Record the article's asset license and embedded font
   provenance, and the person-splat outputs.
5. **tools/sound-sources.json:** preserve the 252 research candidate records as candidates and add
   selected output mappings for all 85 MP3s, including the 69 F3 rows. Align source IDs, authors,
   license/version, preview/archive member, cut/encoding and hashes with tools/assets.json and
   src/sound-credits.js. Keep the piano pack/individual-note relationship explicit. **CREDITS.md:**
   correct the obsolete Sound B reference to an assets.json `sounds` field; the actual field is
   `soundSamples`. **src/toy-sounds.js; src/sound-credits.js:** no author/license disagreement was
   found; update these only if a lane changes the selection.
6. **tools/fetch-flags.mjs; assets/flags/flags.json:** preserve complete author text, represent
   absent author information explicitly, and record source revision/hash plus local output hash.
   Recheck the 39 blocked flag pages and the seven other blocked Commons file pages listed in F4
   when access is available, then update affected manifests/credits only from the live file-specific
   evidence. Do not infer deletion or changed licensing from HTTP 429.
7. **assets/toys/chess-set/chess-set.sog and chess-set-lite.sog:** either retain these unused model
   outputs with an explicit archived-source record/credit or remove them in the owning lane; the
   current chess toy is kit-built. **LICENSES.md:** enumerate the Foxit BSD 3-clause, Liberation GPL
   v2/font exception, Adobe CMap BSD 3-clause and ICC CC0 licenses with their exact shipped license
   paths. Replace the stale two-MIT-libraries opening. Keep all 10 direct devDependency entries.
8. **vendor/pdfjs/standard_fonts/; CLAUDE.md (owner policy only if authorized):** resolve the
   14-font allowlist conflict through an allowed replacement or an explicitly approved policy for
   the runtime resource bundle. If keeping Liberation, establish its corresponding source
   distribution/offer requirement for these files and provide the required material. Do not silently
   edit CLAUDE.md as a routine audit fix.
9. **vendor/onnxruntime-web/; LICENSES.md:** determine which third-party notices the exact 1.30.0
   WASM binary requires, ship those notices, and document them. Record the review's build/version so
   it does not equate every upstream platform dependency with this build.
10. **tools/pic-samples.mjs; tools/make-song-sample.mjs; tools/make-thumbs.mjs and asset preparation
    tools:** emit source/output identity receipts and original-asset license declarations into the
    selected manifests. Keep source-derived thumbnail licenses at least as specific as their parent
    asset. Any newly chosen BY-SA asset must have its own versioned notice beside it and preserve
    that license for converted files and thumbnails.
